> ### **`SIX OF THE NINE FACTS COUNSEL NEEDS ARE `[UNKNOWN]`. THAT IS ITSELF THE MOST IMPORTANT INPUT TO THE OPINION, AND IT MUST BE DISCLOSED AS AN UNKNOWN RATHER THAN LEFT FOR COUNSEL TO DISCOVER.`**

## VIII.5 The consequence tree

| Branch | **If the answer is…** | **Then…** |
|---|---|---|
| **A** | **Unpaid status materially reduces personal exposure** | **`D-8`'s boundary can be set later; `VB-5` remains a publication duty rather than an urgency. **This branch must not be assumed, and if reached, must state which dimensions it applies to and which it does not**** |
| **B** | **Unpaid status is irrelevant to exposure** | **`D-8` becomes urgent: **the point at which each role ceases to be volunteerable must be stated and published before the role is performed again**. `GD-10` moves from `REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION` to a protective obligation owed to four named people** |
| **C** | **Exposure attaches to the person who acts where no body exists** | **Every act in CEA `XX.2` acquires a personal consequence for its performer, and `GF-1`'s thirteen `UNASSIGNED` cells become thirteen places where **nobody may act** rather than thirteen places where anybody may. **This is the branch that most changes the architecture's operating reality, and it changes nothing in its text**** |
| **D** | **Exposure differs by classification** | **`SR-02` becomes a protective instrument and not only a compliance one, and its Tier-0 status is confirmed from a second direction** |
| **E** | **Exposure differs by legal structure** | **`SR-01` acquires criterion **FC-E1** in its personal-liability limb, and counsel's comparison must state per option what the four role-holders' exposure would be** |
| **F** | **No indemnity is available from any candidate structure without insurance** | **`SR-04` and `L1 … L15` become gating for the four roles rather than for the pilot, and **`L6`**'s own warning becomes operative: *"Personal exposure deters capable people from governance roles."* **The architecture would then be asking four people to hold uninsurable, unindemnified exposure for no payment — and `D-2` requires that this be said to them in those words**** |

| Rule carried forward | **Content** |
|---|---|
| **G-15** | **Every corpus option survives the merge.** Where two instruments use different words for what may or may not be the same thing — *"nonprofit entity"*, *"nonprofit/social arm"*, *"social arm"*, *"trust"*, *"society"*, *"Section 8 type structure"* — **the words are kept side by side and the question of whether they denote the same legal object is referred to counsel** |
| **G-16** | **Absent categories are surfaced, not endorsed.** `OPT-E`, `OPT-F`, `OPT-G` carry `NOT PRESENT IN CORPUS` and every differentiated cell for them defaults to `UNKNOWN FROM CURRENT CORPUS` |
| **G-17** | **The option list is open.** *"OPT-A … OPT-J is a schedule of options for counsel to complete, not a menu to choose from. Counsel may add forms, exclude forms as legally unavailable, or find that two listed options are the same object"* — **and the matrix must then be re-run** |
| **G-18** | **The matrix records source statements, not legal capability.** A `PC` cell never means *"this form can lawfully do this"*; a `PR` cell never means *"this form cannot"* |
| **G-19** | **`OPT-E`, `OPT-F`, `OPT-G` may never carry `PC`, `PR` or `SS`**, because no corpus statement exists that could support one |
| **G-20** | **Every `SS` cell carries the mandatory sentence: `STRUCTURAL SEPARATION MAY BE REQUIRED — COUNSEL TO DETERMINE`** |

## IX.4 The gap analysis — mandate §9's attributes against `FC-01 … FC-33`

**[P]** The mandate requires the framework to identify twenty-eight attributes for every candidate structure. **Twenty are already covered by the existing criterion set. Eight are not, and the eight are precisely the economic ones — because `FC-01 … FC-33` were built for the four institutional pillars, and the economic architecture did not exist when they were written.**

## IX.6 `GC-01` — the conflict counsel must be shown, and must not resolve

**[P]** `[SOURCE]` — **[S-G:156, 491]**; **[S-K(E): GC-01 `UNRESOLVED`]**. **Both texts are preserved, per EB-5. This brief does not reconcile them.**

| Text | **Content** | **Source** |
|---|---|---|
| **Text 1** | **The eight-priority waterfall is `APPROVED ARCHITECTURE` and a reserved matter.** Priority 4 is *"Founder compensation / approved cap"*; priority 8 is *"Residual surplus / founder upside"* | **[S: Ch.18]**; **[S-D: LDR-C16]** *"APPROVED — THE CONTROL"*; **[S-A: A-14 §13.3]**; **[S-D: LDR-C26]**; **[appendix.txt:6301–6321, 6362–6363]** |
| **Text 2** | **The not-for-profit forms carry *"Restrictions on profit distribution and founder upside"* and *"may not fit commercial waterfall without separate structure"*** | **[S: App. A §3.2 row 3]** |
| **The conflict** | **Priorities 4 and 8 may be incapable of existing in OPT-A, OPT-B or OPT-C. **The `SS` cells at FC-E2, FC-E3 and FC-E8 are the matrix expression of the same conflict**** | **[S-G:156]** — *"the sharpest unrecorded conflict in the corpus, and it is structural"* |
| **What is already recorded around it** | **`CF-16`** founder compensation is a conflict *"that cannot be managed"*; **`SR-20` / `LDR-U16`** is `AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED (beneficiary recusal)` and requires *"a recorded position, even if nil"*; **CEA X.6** records an adverse incentive finding and performs no reordering; **CEA GD-08** makes any change a Tier-1 amendment | — |
| **What would move the whole assessment** | ***"(2) a human decision on GC-01 — whether priorities 4 and 8 of the waterfall are to be preserved in the chosen form, or referred [to a separate structure]"*** **[S-G:168]** | — |
| **Treatment here** | **`GC-01 REMAINS UNRESOLVED.`** Counsel is asked to state, per option, **which priorities the form can carry and which it cannot**. Counsel is **not** asked to choose. **The choice is `REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION` + `REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION (TIER-1)` + `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION`** | **EB-5, mandate §32** |

## IX.7 What counsel must deliver, per option

**[P]** The deliverable is a **comparison**, not a recommendation. **One row per option, one column per criterion, every cell answered, and no cell left as "generally" or "typically".**

| Requirement | **Content** |
|---|---|
| **1** | **All ten options scored against `FC-01 … FC-33` and `FC-E1 … FC-E8`** — forty-one criteria. **Where counsel adds an option under `OPT-J` / Rule G-17, the full set is re-run for it** |
| **2** | **Where an option is legally unavailable for any of the four pillars, that finding is recorded so the option set can be closed** — **OPT-E's own counsel question: *"If not available, that finding should be recorded so the option set can be closed"*** |
| **3** | **Where two listed options are the same legal object, that is stated** — Rule G-15's *"nonprofit entity" / "Section 8 company"* question, and *"whether 'trust' denotes a public charitable trust rather than a private one"* |
| **4** | **Per option: which of the eight waterfall priorities the form can carry, and which it cannot** — **FC-E8**, and the direct answer to `GC-01`'s factual limb |
| **5** | **Per option: whether priority 1 is dischargeable in a weak month, and if not, what the lawful alternative is** — **FC-E5**, **PVR-03** |
| **6** | **Per option: the registrations, identifiers, filings and ongoing obligations it would carry, stated as facts and not as targets — every number marked `CURRENT-LAW PROFESSIONAL VERIFICATION REQUIRED`** |
| **7** | **Per option: the banking and signatory consequences** — **FC-12**, **A-N16 T-1 … T-5**, **SR-03** |
| **8** | **Per option: the insurance availability for the four control roles and for protection activity** — **FC-29**, **L1 … L15**, **SR-04** |
| **9** | **Per option: the data-fiduciary consequence** — **FC-31**, **A-N14**, **SR-05**, **SR-06** |
| **10** | **Per option: what may lawfully cross to or from Krytos, and what may not** — **FC-E7**, **CEA XV.4**, **`H-Q17`**, **`H-Q19`** |
| **11** | **Per option: the dissolution and continuity consequences** — **FC-32**, **FC-33**, **LDR-U19**, **SR-10** |
| **12** | **Every assumption counsel makes, listed. Every conflict between the architecture and the law, surfaced. Every limitation of the opinion, stated** — mandate §28 |
| **13** | **The whole delivered in §XXV's controlled format, so that it can be filed as governance evidence under **[S: Ch.27]** and **[S-F:1975 SR-98]**** |

## IX.8 What this Part refuses to do

| Refusal | **Ground** |
|---|---|
| **It does not select an entity** | Mandate §10, §26; **DA-11**; Rule G-1; CEA **EA-20**. **The chain `OPTION SET → CONSEQUENCE ANALYSIS → PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION → HUMAN DECISION` is not short-circuited by a brief** |
| **It does not rank the options** | **Rule G-3 governs the source's *"strong candidate"* and *"recommended for professional exploration"* labels: all four end in a validation condition, and none is an approval** |
| **It does not add an option of its own** | **Rule G-16. Any form counsel adds is marked `PROFESSIONAL INPUT — NOT CURRENT ARCHITECTURE DECISION`** |
| **It does not state that Sewak Sathi should be registered as anything** | Standing rule: *"Never say 'Sewak Sathi should be registered as X' unless the corpus decided it."* **The corpus has not decided it** |
| **It does not resolve `GC-01`, `UE-24`, `CF-16`, `SR-20` or `LDR-U19`** | EB-5; **DA-15**, **DA-16**; CEA **EA-24** |
| **It does not treat the founder's assertions as a structure** | **FA-1 … FA-7**; Rule **K-4**; **DA-30**. **OPT-I's conceptual state is a description in a source, not a legal arrangement** |

>
> **Tests **T-1 … T-5 all fail; 0 of 5 pass**. The block is now **confirmed by an express negative** rather than merely unproven: *there is no legal person to hold an account.* **[S-K(E): §XVII.2 test 3, Part XII]****

## X.2 The five tests, and what each requires

**[P]** `[SOURCE]` — **[S-K(E): Part XII, T-1 … T-5]**. **Each is reproduced with its finding and its dependency. None is re-run, re-scored or softened.**

| Test | **Question** | **Finding** | **What must exist before it can pass** | **Who supplies it** |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **T-1** | **Is there an entity capable of holding an account?** | **`FAILS`** — `LDR-U08` `UNRESOLVED`; **N-2 open**; *"There is no legal person to incur cost, hold an account, employ anyone or be audited"* **[S-G:2323]**. **And the failure is now evidenced rather than unproven** | **`SR-01` decided on a counsel opinion** | **Corporate counsel + the founder** |
| **T-2** | **Is there signatory authority?** | **`FAILS`** — Krytos has no officers, no board, no delegation (**GA-01 … GA-12: 0 of 12**); Sewak Sathi's CEO appointment is a reserved matter with an undetermined holder. **FA-6 declares control of Krytos, not signatory authority over any account** | **An office, an appointment, and a delegation — each requiring T-1 first** | **Governance decision + counsel** |
| **T-3** | **Is there continuity of authority?** | **`FAILS`** — `LDR-U19`: *"Not named anywhere in the source hierarchy"* **[S-D:4594]**; **[S-A: A-14 S5]** contemplates *"two regimes in parallel"*. **FA-6 makes the single point of failure concrete: one natural person is the declared owner and controller, and no succession instrument exists** | **A continuity rule and a succession instrument — `SR-10`, `A-N17`, *"preferably before the first hire"*** | **Founder decision + counsel** |
| **T-4** | **Is there a mandate?** | **`FAILS`** — **eight proposals, 0 adoptions**, all in instruments downstream of the unresolved row; **[S-B: E-03]** *"UNRESOLVED — no source instrument"* | **A mandate document — `SR-03`'s own evidence requirement** | **Counsel + the institution + governance** |
| **T-5** | **Is there separation of personal and organisational funds?** | **`FAILS`** — **`OC-10`: 0 of 17 asset classes have a stated owner**; `A-N15` custody of reserves and the social-impact fund `UNRESOLVED` **[S-A:1588]**. **FA-4 and FA-6 together make separation impossible today: no body exists to hold organisational funds** | **An asset register with an owner per class, and a custody instrument — `SR-19`** | **Governance decision + counsel + chartered accountant** |

## X.3 The chain from entity to audit trail

**[P]** Mandate §11 requires the chain **entity → bank/account ownership → mandate → signatory → approval → payment initiation → payment verification → accounting record → audit trail**. **Nine links. Each is stated with what it requires, its current state, and who must supply it. No link is skipped, and no link is invented.**

| # | **Link** | **What it requires** | **Current state** | **Supplied by** | **Discipline** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | **Entity** | A legal person capable of holding an account and being an obligor | **`DOES NOT EXIST`** — `SR-01`, `LDR-U08`, FA-4, FA-5, T-1 | **Counsel's opinion, then a human decision, then a constitutive document** | **Legal counsel** |
| **2** | **Bank / account ownership** | An account held **in the entity's name**, not in any person's name; and a stated owner for each of the 17 asset classes | **`DOES NOT EXIST`** — T-1, T-5; **`OC-10`: 0 of 17 asset classes have a stated owner**; **no bank is named anywhere and none may be** | **The entity, once it exists; **`SR-19`** custody instrument** | **Legal counsel + banking input + governance decision** |
| **3** | **Mandate** | A document conferring authority to operate the account, with its scope, limits and duration | **`DOES NOT EXIST`** — T-4: **eight proposals, 0 adoptions**; **`SR-03`'s evidence required is *"the mandate document"*** | **Counsel drafts; the entity adopts; the institution accepts** | **Legal counsel + banking input** |
| **4** | **Signatory** | **Two or more signatories with a continuity rule** — the requirement is `[SOURCE]`, not an invention. **The number above two, the identities, and the classes of authority are not set and may not be invented here** | **`DOES NOT EXIST`** — T-2, T-3; **`LDR-U19`**; **no signatory number is invented (DA-10)** | **Governance appointment + counsel + the institution's know-your-customer process** | **Governance decision + legal counsel + banking input** |
| **5** | **Approval** | A person or body authorised to approve a payment **before** it is initiated, who is not the initiator, not the receiver and not the beneficiary | **`UNASSIGNED`** — CEA **GF-1**: `APPROVES` unassigned in **13 of 16** economic acts; **`LDR-U01`**, **`A-N19`**, **`LDR-U19`** all `AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED`; *"the body that would decide is itself the subject of the decision"* **[S-F:159]** | **Governance decision — `GD-01`. **This link does not require an entity to be **designed**, and Part XXVI treats its design as parallel work**** | **Governance decision + internal control design** |
| **6** | **Payment initiation** | A person who executes the payment under the mandate, within limits, against an approval | **`BLOCKED`** — **`C-15`**: no external payment can lawfully or operationally be made; **`PP-04`** | **The mandate and the signatory — links 3 and 4** | **Internal control design + banking input** |
| **7** | **Payment verification** | A person who confirms that what was paid matches what was approved, who is **not** the initiator | **`BLOCKED`** — CEA **COI-1**: the Funds Manager may not approve, initiate, receive and verify the same payment; **EA-16**'s four-hand rule; **[appendix.txt:6925–6948]** Diagram 20A | **Segregation design — `GD-11`, and CEA **F-11** is open because eleven people cannot supply four independent hands** | **Internal control design + governance decision** |
| **8** | **Accounting record** | A record carrying all eight fields — `WHO → WHAT → WHEN → WHY → AMOUNT → AUTHORITY → EVIDENCE → STATUS` — for every movement | **`DOES NOT EXIST`** — CEA **F-17** is open; **REC-01 … REC-17** are specified and **9 of 17 have `AUTHORITY` `UNASSIGNED` or `BLOCKED`**; **no accounting system exists and none may be built** | **Internal control design; **paper is sufficient** *(CEA §XXI.1)*; retention periods from counsel — **`SR-06`, `DR-9`, PVR-20** | **Accounting opinion + internal control design** |
| **9** | **Audit trail** | A trail that lets an independent person reconstruct any payment from its approval to its record, without asking the person who made it | **`DOES NOT EXIST`** — CEA **AF-2**: all twelve audits currently return `NO POPULATION`, which is **not** `NO EXCEPTIONS`; **`AU-11` is external-only because internal independence is unachievable for founder compensation** | **Internal control design now; an auditor once a population exists** | **Internal control design + accounting opinion** |

> ### **`NINE LINKS. EIGHT DO NOT EXIST OR ARE BLOCKED. ONE — LINK 5, THE APPROVAL DESIGN — CAN BE WORKED ON TODAY WITHOUT AN ENTITY, AND IT IS THE ONLY ONE.`**

## X.4 What must not be invented

**[P]** Mandate §11, adopted exactly. **Each item is listed with the reason it cannot be invented and the state the corpus actually records.**

| Item | **State** | **Why it cannot be invented** |
|---|---|---|
| **Signatory count** | **The **requirement** is `[SOURCE]`: *"two or more signatories with a continuity rule"* **[S-A: §22 N16]**, *"≥2 signatories"* **[S-F:974 P0-C]**. **The **actual** number, the identities and the authority classes are `UNKNOWN`** | **`DA-10`: *"No signatory number is invented."* A count above the stated minimum is a governance decision, not a drafting choice** |

| **Payment provider** | **`NOT NAMED ANYWHERE`** | **A payment system is prohibited: **`SR-77`** is *"Tier 0 because it is a refusal"*; CEA **EA-21**** |
| **Accounting system** | **`NOT NAMED ANYWHERE`** | **No software may be designed, authorised or implied — CEA **§XXI.1**; mandate §26 of the CEA task** |

| **No single person may propose, approve, sign, receive, authorise, verify and audit the same transaction** | **CEA **XX.1**'s eight governance functions, with the combination rules** | **`[SOURCE]` — and the matrix shows it is currently violated by necessity, not by choice** |
| **Separation of personal and organisational funds** | **T-5's subject. **`OC-10`: 0 of 17 asset classes have a stated owner**** | **`[SOURCE]` as a requirement · `UNSATISFIED` as a fact · **the asset register can be written today, on paper, with `UNKNOWN` in the owner column until counsel answers**** |
| **Every movement recorded with eight fields** | **CEA **REC-01 … REC-17**, **DR-1 … DR-9**, on paper** | **`[SOURCE]` — **available now**; **ID-13** is one of only two CEA dependency steps not blocked** |

# PART XI — ECONOMIC WATERFALL: LEGAL AND ACCOUNTING VALIDATION

## XI.1 The principle, preserved verbatim and unreordered

**[P]** `[SOURCE]` — **[appendix.txt:6296]**, `APPROVED ARCHITECTURE`; **[S-D: LDR-C16]** *"APPROVED — THE CONTROL"*; CEA **RT-01**, **Part X**.

> ### **`THE PRIORITY ORDER IS THE CONTROL; PERCENTAGES ARE A PLANNING TOOL.`**
>
> ***"The source blueprint principle is preserved. A percentage table applied blindly to gross revenue can break the system because it may pay discretionary allocations before wages, taxes or direct costs. A priority waterfall defines what must be protected first and what can fall to zero in weak periods. Percentages may help planning, but they do not override the priority order, law or final approved finance policy."*** **[appendix.txt:6296–6300]**
>
> **This brief does not reorder the waterfall, create a percentage, set a salary, define a margin or propose a reserve target. Mandate §12 is absolute. What it does is separate eight things that the word "priority" is currently doing at once.**

| The eight priorities, exactly as the source states them | **Anchor** |
|---|---|
| **1. WORKER COMPENSATION · 2. STATUTORY DUES WHERE APPLICABLE · 3. DIRECT BUSINESS / DELIVERY COSTS · 4. FOUNDER COMPENSATION / APPROVED CAP · 5. ORGANISATION SERVICE FEE · 6. RESERVES / WAGE-PROTECTION BUFFER · 7. SOCIAL-IMPACT CONTRIBUTION WHEN CONDITIONS ARE MET · 8. RESIDUAL SURPLUS / FOUNDER UPSIDE** | **[appendix.txt:6301–6321]** Diagram 14A; definitions at **[appendix.txt:6348–6363]** |

## XI.2 The eight distinctions, applied priority by priority

**[P]** Mandate §12: *"architectural priority, legal enforceability, accounting treatment, tax treatment, cash availability, contractual obligation, governance approval and implementation formula — these are different things."* **They are separated here for the first time. Every cell is a question or a recorded state; none is an answer.**

| Priority | **Architectural position** | **Legal enforceability** | **Accounting treatment** | **Tax treatment** | **Cash availability** | **Contractual obligation** | **Governance approval** | **Implementation formula** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **1 Worker compensation** | **First. Non-suspendable — `[S-D: INV-2]`. Indivisible with priority 2 in a weak period — CEA `JT-1 … JT-5`** | **`PVR-03`, `PLV-01 … PLV-16` — whether an entitlement exists at all turns on classification (`SR-02`)** | **Expense or a liability; the accrual's character is `PVR-04`** | **Withholding, contributions, reporting — `PVR-07`; whether a net payment may be made while withholding is undischarged is CEA `JT-2`** | **`REV-2` / `REV-3` — the only priority that cannot be met from `REV-1`** | **None exists. No contract, no capacity — `PRV-03`, `PLV-07`** | **`UNASSIGNED` — CEA `GF-1`; the formula's approval is `REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION` + National + `[V]`** | **`DOES NOT EXIST` — `[S-B: PC-01]` *"the formula does not exist"*; CEA **GF-3**, **V-01 … V-03**** |
| **2 Statutory dues where applicable** | **Second, and joint with priority 1. *"Statutory dues are never reduced by this rule"* — CEA `JT-3`** | **The source's own qualifier governs: *"where applicable under legal/tax/employment structure"*. **Which obligations exist is unknown until `SR-01` and `SR-02`**** | **Accrual on the applicable basis; the base and rate are `UNKNOWN`** | **`PVR-06`, `PVR-07`, Part XIV — `REQUIRES QUALIFIED TAX ADVICE`. Every rate and threshold is `CURRENT-LAW PROFESSIONAL VERIFICATION REQUIRED`** | **Same collection as priority 1 — `JT-1`** | **Statutory, not contractual** | **`UNASSIGNED`; the signatory is `BLOCKED` — `GD-05`** | **Cannot be computed today — CEA `REC-10` carries `UNKNOWN`** |
| **3 Direct business / delivery costs** | **Third. Non-deferrable for costs already incurred; *"must be restructured, paused or closed"* if it cannot be met** | **Owed to third parties under contracts that do not exist and cannot be made — `FC-08`, `PRV-03`** | **Cost attribution between venture, organisation and mission is the `G-05` / `AG-02` boundary; capitalisation is `PVR-18`** | **Input credits, deductibility and the point of supply — Part XIV** | **Usually incurred **before** collection: CEA **X.3 determinant 4** — priority 3 is an allocation rule for costs already incurred, not a funding rule for costs about to be incurred. **This is the working-capital gap, `HC-3`** | **Would be contractual** | **Venture approval is `UNASSIGNED`; cost classification is human, AI may flag only** | **Cost record **REC-05** with venture, pool, priority and category** |
| **4 Founder compensation / approved cap** | **Fourth — **above** the organisation's own funding and **above** reserves. CEA **X.6** records an adverse incentive finding and performs no reordering** | **`CF-16`: a conflict *"that cannot be managed"*; `SR-20` / `LDR-U16` `AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED (beneficiary recusal)`, requiring *"a recorded position, even if nil"*. **Whether it may exist at all is `GC-01` and **FC-E3**** | **Whether it is remuneration, a fee, a distribution or something else — `PVR-08`, CEA **SEP-4**** | **`REQUIRES QUALIFIED TAX ADVICE`. **The character determines the regime**** | **After three prior claims** | **None. **No employment or service agreement exists, and none may be invented**** | **`AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED`. The approver would be the beneficiary — **CEA `GF-5`: only an external auditor can examine it**** | **Cap is `[M]` — `V-08`. **Not applicable at Mohalla level** — `[S-B: §8.7]`** |
| **5 Organisation service fee** | **Fifth, explicitly deferrable. **Its recipient is not identified**** | **`H-Q17`: *"Can Sewak Sathi pay Krytos?"* — `UNKNOWN`, `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION`; *"Paying an unconstituted construct is not possible"* **[S-K(E):854]**. **And CEA **GD-02** asks which of `C_L1-a/b/c` funds L2** | **Whether it is an internal transfer, an expense or a distribution — and to whom** | **`PVR-09`, Part XIV. **An internal transfer between parts of one entity and a payment to another entity are different tax events**** | **After four prior claims — and **CEA `XII.1`: *"the centre is funded last and spent first"*** | **None** | **`UNASSIGNED`; **the amount is a Tier-1 matter — `V-11`**** | **The source calls it *"allocation to sustain organisational infrastructure after higher-priority items"* **[appendix.txt:6356–6357]**** |
| **6 Reserves / wage-protection buffer** | **Sixth. **CEA `X.3` determinant 9: the corpus's own characterisation is *"discretionary in the short run, fatal to skip in the long run"*** | **Whether a reserve is a legal obligation, a governance commitment or nothing — `PVR-04` by analogy** | **An appropriation, a liability or a restriction — and whether it is a **restricted fund** is `FC-10`, `FC-11`, `SR-19`** | **Whether an appropriation has any tax consequence — Part XIV** | **Arrives after four prior claims, so **CEA `XIV.4`: a reserve cannot fund the first weak month**. **`P-6`, the seeded float, is `PROPOSED` only**** | **None** | **`UNASSIGNED` — `GD-03`; custody `SR-19` `EXPLICITLY UNRESOLVED`, *decide with SR-03*** | **Adequacy expressed in **periods of coverage of the joint tier**, not in money — CEA **XIV.3**; the integer is `V-07`, `GD-09`** |

| **8 Residual surplus / founder upside** | **Eighth and last. *"Remaining surplus after priority obligations and caps as defined by approved structure"* **[appendix.txt:6362–6363]**** | **`GC-01`'s centre: *"restrictions on profit distribution and founder upside"* for the not-for-profit forms; **`FC-E2`, `FC-E3`, `FC-E8`**. **Leader equity, profit share and ownership stakes are prohibited — `[S-D:2999]`** | **Whether a residual is distributable at all, and to whom — `PVR-09`** | **`REQUIRES QUALIFIED TAX ADVICE`. **CEA `XV.5`: *"until an enterprise with equity and distributable profits exists, priority 8 is a residual with no entitled recipient"*** | **After seven prior claims** | **None** | **`AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED`; **CEA `GF-5`**** | **`V-08`. **No mechanism exists, and none is created here**** |

## XI.3 The two frozen ordering questions, and what counsel must determine about them

**[P]** `[SOURCE]` — CEA **X.6**, **X.7**, **GD-07**, **GD-08**. **Both are Tier-1 amendments to an `APPROVED` order. Neither is reordered by this brief.**

| Question | **The architectural position** | **What counsel must determine** | **What counsel must not do** |
|---|---|---|---|
| **`GD-08` — does founder compensation remain at priority 4?** | **CEA **X.6** records that position 4 places the founder ahead of the organisation's own funding *(5)* and ahead of the reserve that protects workers *(6)*, and that this creates a structural interest in underfunding the control system. **It also records the fair counter-argument: the corpus limits it — *"capped, and not applicable at Mohalla level"* **[S-B: §8.7]** — so at the only level that will operate for some time, priority 4 pays nothing** | **Whether the position is lawful in each candidate form; whether it is taxable as remuneration or as a distribution; whether it is compatible with the not-for-profit forms at all (**FC-E3**, **GC-01**); and what a *"cap"* must contain to be effective** | **Decide the position. That is `REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION` + `REQUIRES GOVERNANCE DECISION (TIER-1)`** |
| **`GD-07` — where does reinvestment sit?** | **CEA **X.7** specifies three candidates — **RI-1** after reserves, **RI-2** after the social contribution, **RI-3** inside priority 3 as a capitalised direct cost — with consequences, and selects none. **RI-3 is noted as the only candidate requiring no amendment, and as the one that widens the `C_var` / `C_L1` boundary where `G-05` expense-shifting lives** | **The accounting and tax treatment of capability spending — expensed or capitalised (**PVR-18**); whether RI-3's boundary can be policed by a written attribution rule; and whether any candidate is unavailable in a given form** | **Select a position. That is a Tier-1 amendment** |

## XI.4 What this Part refuses to create

| Refusal | **Ground** |
|---|---|
| **No reordering of the eight priorities** | Mandate §12; CEA **EA-7**, **X.1**; **[S-B: §20.2]** — the waterfall priority order is *"constitutional; v1.3-A Tier-1 candidate"* |
| **No percentage, of anything, at any priority** | Mandate §12, §24; CEA **EA-9**; **[appendix.txt:6367–6369]** — *"This specification does not adopt those as final Sewak Sathi Sanstha percentages"*; **SR-22** — the illustrative percentages are *"EXPLICITLY UNRESOLVED — illustrative only"*, *"Never publish as policy"* |
| **No salary, rate, cap, multiplier or margin** | Mandate §24; CEA **V-01 … V-03**, **V-08**; **SR-15** — *"EXPLICITLY UNRESOLVED — pilot measures it"*, Tier 2, *"do not decide before measurement"* |
| **No reserve target, size or coverage figure** | Mandate §24; CEA **XIV.3**, **V-07**, **GD-09** — adequacy is expressed in periods of coverage and the integer is unset |
| **No statutory rate, threshold or interval stated as fact** | Mandate §24: any such number is marked **`CURRENT-LAW PROFESSIONAL VERIFICATION REQUIRED`** and is not treated as an architectural assumption |
| **No claim that the waterfall is lawful, compliant or operable** | **`P0-C`** requires that *"the waterfall is operable"*; it is not, and this brief does not make it so |

# PART XII — FOUNDER, KRYTOS AND CAPITAL SEPARATION

## XII.1 What this Part does, and what it refuses

| This Part does | **This Part refuses** |
|---|---|
| **Preserves the seven Krytos and founder statements exactly as the CEA holds them — as **assertions** — and names the legal question each one raises** | **Convert any assertion into legal-person status, ownership proof, corporate control, fiduciary identity, parent/subsidiary status, tax status, jurisdiction or authority — mandate §2** |
| **Preserves the eight separations, and states what document each one would need in order to be enforceable rather than merely stated** | **Perform any separation. A separation that no instrument enforces is a description, not a control** |
| **Preserves the ten flow pairings with their preconditions, and the result **zero permissible · two impossible**** | **Invent a salary, dividend, profit share, fee, licence, royalty, transfer or agreement — mandate §13** |
| **Preserves **`ZERO CASH INVESTMENT IS NOT ZERO ECONOMIC COST`** and classifies, across fifteen capital kinds, what can and cannot yet be claimed** | **Claim any deduction, allowance, valuation, credit, cost recovery or contribution recognition. "Claimed" in this Part means **stated as an architectural fact**, never as a tax or accounting position** |

## XII.2 The seven assertions, and the question each raises

**[P]** `[SOURCE]` — CEA **§XV.2**, **FA-1 … FA-7**; mandate §2; **Rules FA-R1 … FA-R4** at §IV.4 of this brief. **The middle column is reproduced from the CEA without amendment. The right column is this brief's contribution: each assertion is converted into a question for a named discipline, and into nothing else.**

| ID | **The assertion — unchanged** | **Its status** | **The question it raises, and who must answer it** |
|---|---|---|---|
| **FA-1** | **Krytos is the Virtual Brain** | **A functional/architectural description of an intended role. **Not a legal person, not a corporate name, not a registered entity**** | **Is "Krytos" capable of being a legal person, a registered name, a trade mark, or nothing at all? **Corporate counsel**, with a name-availability and trade-mark search. **`CQ-28`**** |
| **FA-2** | **Krytos is the ecosystem head** | **An intended position in an intended hierarchy. **Not a legally recognised parent, not a holding relationship**** | **Does any parent/holding relationship exist in law between anything and anything, and if one is wanted, what instruments create it? **Corporate counsel**. **`OPT-I` is the only candidate the corpus describes as the present conceptual state, and CEA **UE-24** blocks it. **`CQ-29`**** |
| **FA-3** | **India jurisdiction is intended** | **A stated intention as to future forum. **Not a completed registration, not a choice of law already made, not an address**** | **Which state's law governs each candidate form, and does state heterogeneity change the answer to `SR-01`? **Corporate counsel + chartered accountant** — CEA **FC-E6** records *"state-level registration and compliance heterogeneity"* for `OPT-B` and `OPT-C`. **`CQ-34`**** |
| **FA-4** | **No constitutive document exists** | **A statement of absence — **the most consequential fact in the corpus**. Not a formality awaiting completion** | **Not a question but the precondition of every question. **What is the minimum constitutive document set for each candidate form, and in what order? **Corporate counsel**. **`CQ-01`, `CQ-02`, `CQ-03`, `CQ-04`**** |
| **FA-5** | **No legal work has been done** | **A statement of absence. **Not *"legal work is unnecessary"*** | **What professional work is required before any economic act may occur? **This brief is the answer to that question and nothing more. Parts XIX and XXIII**** |
| **FA-6** | **Vasu Gupta will own and control Krytos** | **A forward-looking intention in the future tense. **Not present ownership, not a shareholding, not a membership, not a trust deed, not a directorship**** | **Three separate questions, which ordinary usage collapses into one — and mandate §6 requires them to be held apart: **(i)** can ownership exist at all in a form that has no owners *(a society has members, a trust has trustees, a Section 8 company has no shareholders)*? **(ii)** can control be conferred, and by what instrument? **(iii)** can one natural person hold both in the same body that also controls a not-for-profit operating entity? **Corporate counsel + governance counsel**. **`CQ-29`, `CQ-30`, `CQ-35`, and §VI.3's three entity-independent limbs `PLV-12`, `PLV-13`, `PLV-14`**** |
| **FA-7** | **The current architecture is being designed now** | **A statement about the instrument. **Not authority to make legal determinations**** | **None. **FA-7 exists to prevent the other six from being read as decisions. **Rule **FA-R3**: designing a structure is not approving it**** |

## XII.3 The eight separations, and what would make each enforceable

**[P]** `[SOURCE]` — CEA **§XV.3**, **SEP-1 … SEP-8**; mandate §13. **State column unchanged. The new column states the instrument that would convert a stated separation into an operative one — as a question, never as a draft.**

| ID | **Separation** | **The collapse it prevents** | **CEA state — unchanged** | **What would make it enforceable, and who determines it** |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **SEP-1** | **The founder's *person* from the founder's *roles*** | **Self-dealing; **`CF-16` — a conflict *"that cannot be managed"*; `AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED (beneficiary recusal)`**** | **`SEPARATED — NO INSTRUMENT EXISTS TO ENFORCE IT`** | **A conflicts policy, a recusal rule with a named non-beneficiary decider, and a register of interests. **Governance counsel drafts; **`GD-01`** supplies the decider. **`CQ-14`**** |
| **SEP-2** | **Krytos from Sewak Sathi Sanstha** | **Treating a description of an ecosystem as a group structure — forbidden assumptions 1, 2 and 3** | **`SEPARATED — RELATIONSHIP `UNKNOWN — DECISION REQUIRED`` · **`STRUCTURAL SEPARATION MAY BE REQUIRED — COUNSEL TO DETERMINE`**** | **A written decision on whether any relationship is wanted, then the instrument that creates it — a holding structure, a services agreement, a licence, or nothing. **Corporate counsel**; **`FC-E7`, `FC-E8`, `OPT-H`, `OPT-I`. `CQ-29`, `CQ-35`**** |
| **SEP-3** | **Ownership from control** | **Treating an assertion of future control as present ownership — forbidden assumption 4** | **`SEPARATED — NEITHER EXISTS IN DOCUMENT`** | **Two documents, not one: a title instrument and a governance/delegation instrument. **Corporate counsel + governance counsel**. **`PLV-12`, `PLV-13`, `CQ-30`, `CQ-35`**** |
| **SEP-4** | **Compensation for work from return on capital** | **Paying a dividend and calling it a fee, or paying a fee and calling it a dividend** | **`SEPARATED IN THE WATERFALL — TAX TREATMENT `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION``** | **A characterisation opinion, then a written agreement of the correct kind, then the withholding and reporting treatment. **Chartered accountant + tax counsel**; **`PVR-08`, `PVR-09`. `CQ-31`, `CQ-32`**** |
| **SEP-5** | **Contribution *in* from extraction *out*** | **Netting the two — which is how **`AG-10`** capture happens invisibly** | **`SEPARATED — `AG-10` APPLIES TO EVERY CONTRIBUTION IN`** | **A contribution policy stating amount, purpose, restriction, whether any claim arises, and full disclosure — with a prohibition on netting. **Governance counsel + chartered accountant**; **`C_L1-a`, `GD-02`. `CQ-15`**** |
| **SEP-6** | **Value created by the founder's work from value created by members' work** | **Founder upside computed from member-generated revenue — **`MLM-2`** inverted** | **`SEPARATED — ATTRIBUTION MECHANISM DOES NOT EXIST`** | **An attribution rule with a record behind it. **This is the only separation that needs a data design rather than a legal instrument — and CEA **§XXI.1** permits it on paper. **Internal control design + accounting opinion**. **`CQ-33`**** |
| **SEP-7** | **The Virtual Brain / ecosystem concept from a legal person** | **Treating **FA-1** and **FA-2** as evidence of existence — **Rule K-4**, `ASSERTION ≠ DOCUMENT`**** | **`SEPARATED BY RULE K-4`** | **Nothing. **SEP-7 is a rule of evidence, not a control, and it is enforced by this brief's own §IV. **`NO SOURCE → NO FACT → NO CLOSURE`**** |
| **SEP-8** | **What is architecturally desirable from what is legally permissible** | **A design document functioning as a legal opinion — **EA-26**, §19 of the delta-audit brief** | **`SEPARATED — THE STANDING RULE OF THIS INSTRUMENT`** | **Nothing. **SEP-8 is why this brief says **`NOT LEGAL ADVICE`** at the top of page one and at §XXX.4. **Mandate §25**** |

## XII.4 The ten flow pairings — zero permissible, two impossible

**[P]** `[SOURCE]` — CEA **§XV.4**; mandate §13. **All ten directional rows are reproduced with their preconditions unchanged. The fourth column names the discipline that would remove each block.**

| # | **Pairing** | **Direction / character** | **Preconditions before any movement — unchanged** | **CEA state** | **Discipline that removes the block** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | **Sewak Sathi → workers** | **Priority 1** | **`SR-01`** a legal person capable of being an obligor · a bank mandate (**`A-N16`**) · worker classification determined **`[V]`** · the formula published (**`D-1`**) · verification independent (**COI-2**)** | **`BLOCKED — A-N16, SR-01`** | **Corporate counsel + labour counsel + chartered accountant + internal control design** |
| **2** | **Sewak Sathi → founder** | **Priority 4** | **All of #1 **plus**: the approving authority resolved with beneficiary recusal **`CF-16`** · a cap approved **`[M]`** · verified work evidenced · **`REQUIRES QUALIFIED TAX ADVICE`** · the priority-4 ordering question at §XI.3 addressed** | **`BLOCKED — AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED`** | **Governance counsel + tax counsel + chartered accountant** |
| **3** | **Founder → Sewak Sathi** | **Contribution** | **`AG-10`** capture: amount, purpose, restriction, whether it creates any claim, and full disclosure · **no member may be required to contribute (`D-3`)** · `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION` on whether a contribution creates debt, capital or a gift** | **`PERMITTED ONLY AS A GOVERNED, DISCLOSED, VOLUNTARY CONTRIBUTION — NO MECHANISM EXISTS`** | **Governance counsel + chartered accountant + tax counsel** |
| **4** | **Sewak Sathi → Krytos** | **Any** | **Both sides must be legal persons. **Krytos has no constitutive document (**FA-4**). **There is no counterparty**** | **`IMPOSSIBLE — NO COUNTERPARTY`** · `STRUCTURAL SEPARATION MAY BE REQUIRED — COUNSEL TO DETERMINE`** | **Corporate counsel — and only after `FA-4` is cured** |
| **5** | **Krytos → Sewak Sathi** | **Any** | **Same as #4** | **`IMPOSSIBLE — NO COUNTERPARTY`** · `STRUCTURAL SEPARATION MAY BE REQUIRED — COUNSEL TO DETERMINE`** | **Corporate counsel — and only after `FA-4` is cured** |
| **6** | **Sewak Sathi → ventures** | **Capital, float, tooling** | **A venture must be capable of holding anything. **Ventures are activity categories, **not entities** — and creating an entity per venture is prohibited (**EA-26**: no entity selection)**** | **`NOT APPLICABLE — VENTURES ARE NOT PERSONS`** | **None. This pairing is a category error and is not cured by any opinion** |
| **7** | **Ventures → Sewak Sathi** | **Revenue, priority 5** | **`SR-01`** · **`A-N16`** · an approved category · a customer acceptance · a reconciliation** | **`BLOCKED — A-N16, SR-01`** | **Corporate counsel + chartered accountant + internal control design** |
| **8** | **Founder → Krytos** | **Capital, IP, effort** | **`FA-6`** is a future intention. **No share, no deed, no assignment, no valuation**** | **`NO INSTRUMENT EXISTS`** | **Corporate counsel + IP counsel + valuer** |
| **9** | **Krytos → founder** | **Upside, dividend, fee** | **An entity, an ownership document, a distributable surplus, a tax characterisation and an authority — **none exist**** | **`IMPOSSIBLE`** | **Corporate counsel + tax counsel + governance counsel — and only after four separate absences are cured** |
| **10** | **Founder → workers** | **Any** | **Would bypass every control in the architecture and create a personal dependency relationship with members** | **`PROHIBITED — `D-3`, X-8`** | **None. **This is a permanent prohibition, not a block: CEA **J-11**, **MLM-3**, **MLM-5**** |

> ### **`TEN PAIRINGS. ZERO PERMITTED TODAY. TWO IMPOSSIBLE — `Krytos ↔ Sewak Sathi` AND `Krytos → founder` — EXPRESSED IN THREE DIRECTIONAL ROWS. TWO PROHIBITED PERMANENTLY. ONE A CATEGORY ERROR. THE REST BLOCKED PENDING `SR-01` AND `A-N16`.`**
>
> **The reconciliation of "two impossible" against three `IMPOSSIBLE` rows is recorded as **ER-4** at §III.2. Both numbers are correct at their own level: two *pairings*, three *directional flows*.**

## XII.5 The principle, preserved

**[P]** `[SOURCE]` — CEA **§XVII.1**; mandate §14.

> ### **`ZERO CASH INVESTMENT IS NOT ZERO ECONOMIC COST.`**
>
> **"Zero capital" as a *funding requirement* is testable and, on the current architecture, false. "Zero capital" as an *entry barrier for a member* is true and worth keeping: no member pays to join, participate, advance, be recognised or leave. CEA **J-01** rejects the name; CEA **LC-1 … LC-6** retain the promise. The architecture never describes itself as zero-capital — it describes itself as **zero-member-fee and capital-dependent** (**LC-6**, marked `NEW — CURRENT SEWAK ARCHITECTURE`).**
>
> **Mandate §3 is also observed: **`Zero-Capital` is not an established fact about this organisation.** It is a historical label from a document that is `HISTORICAL/REFERENCE ONLY` (**FD-1**) and, in any event, is not in evidence.**

## XII.6 The fifteen capital kinds — what can and cannot yet be claimed

**[P]** CEA **§XVII.2** names **fourteen** hidden capital requirements, **HC-1 … HC-14**. Mandate §14 requires classification across **fifteen**. **The reconciliation is stated rather than hidden: the fifteenth row is supplied here from the CEA's own finding at §XXIV.5 — *"decision latency as an economic cost"* — and is labelled `PROFESSIONAL INPUT — NOT CURRENT ARCHITECTURE DECISION`, as mandate §9 requires of anything this brief adds. It is not added to the CEA.**

> **`CLAIMED` IS DEFINED. **In the two right-hand columns, "claimed" means **stated as an architectural fact** — that the capital is required, that it is unsupplied, and that its absence has a named consequence. **It never means a deduction, an allowance, a credit, a valuation, a cost recovery, a contribution recognition or an accounting entry. **Every tax and accounting consequence of every row is `[UNKNOWN]` and is referred to Part XIV, Part XV and the register at Part XIX.**

| ID | **Capital kind** | **Who supplies it today** | **WHAT CAN BE CLAIMED NOW** | **WHAT CANNOT YET BE CLAIMED** | **Discipline** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **HC-1** | **Constitutive capital** — counsel, drafting, filing, governance documents | **`NOBODY`** | **That it is required; that `SR-01` cannot be decided without it; that it must exist **before** the first collection** | **Any amount, any fee estimate, any tax treatment of professional fees, any statement that it is deductible or capitalisable** | **Corporate counsel + chartered accountant** |
| **HC-2** | **Compliance capital** — registrations, a bank mandate, statutory enrolments, filing capability | **`NOBODY`** | **That `A-N16` remains blocked without it; that no money can be received or paid at all** | **Which registrations apply, their cost, their sequence, or whether any is unnecessary** | **Corporate counsel + chartered accountant + company secretary** |
| **HC-3** | **Working capital** — the gap between incurring a direct cost and collecting for it | **`NOBODY`** | **That priority 3 cannot be met; that a venture growing faster than it collects becomes insolvent; that this is the **`S-1 … S-6`** solvency problem** | **Any float figure, any collection cycle, any credit term, any statement that priority 6 can fund it (**CEA §XIV.4**: it arrives too late)** | **Chartered accountant + treasury/banking input** |
| **HC-4** | **Wage-protection capital** — the buffer that makes priority 1 survive a weak month | **`NOBODY — priority 6 is behind four claims`** | **That the shortfall rule fires in the first weak month; that priorities 1 and 2 are one indivisible joint tier (**JT-1 … JT-5**)** | **Any reserve size, coverage period or target — CEA expresses adequacy in periods of coverage and the integer is **`V-07`, `GD-09`**, unset** | **Chartered accountant + governance decision** |
| **HC-5** | **Human time capital** — the unpaid hours of the eleven roles performing L2 work while also doing L1 | **The role-holders, invisibly** | **That the cost is being borne personally and is not recorded; that this is the **`IST-3`** finding: `EXPOSED, NAMED, MEASURED, UNFUNDED`; that unpaid ≠ no liability (Part XVII)** | **That the time is compensable, quantifiable, recoverable, or capable of being contributed as capital — and **no** volunteer-hour valuation is invented** | **Labour counsel + chartered accountant + insurance broker** |
| **HC-6** | **Skills capital** — sales, pricing, negotiation, delivery quality, financial literacy | **`NOBODY`** | **That every venture is priced by guesswork without it; that **LC-4** requires capability to be acquired by resourcing and sharing before purchase** | **Any training budget, any fee, any statement that training may be charged to a member (**CEA `J-11`: permanent prohibition**)** | **Governance decision + chartered accountant** |
| **HC-7** | **Infrastructure capital** — tools, materials, equipment, workspace, connectivity | **Non-cash resourcing in principle** | **That L3 is unfunded; that a venture cannot deliver what it has sold** | **Any valuation of contributed assets, any depreciation position, any statement of who owns a contributed tool (**`OC-10`: 0 of 17 asset classes have a stated owner**)** | **Chartered accountant + valuer + insurance broker** |
| **HC-8** | **Technology and AI capital** — models, compute, licences, integration, maintenance, and the skill to supervise them | **`NOBODY`** | **That the AI Business Network is an architecture without a funded capability; that **no software may be designed, authorised or implied** (**CEA §XXI.1**, delta-audit §18)** | **Any licence cost, any capitalisation position, any IP ownership statement, any procurement decision** | **Chartered accountant + IP counsel + technology counsel** |
| **HC-9** | **Trust and brand capital** — the acquisition cost of a first customer in a neighbourhood | **Nobody can buy it; it is earned slowly** | **That it is required, that it is not purchasable, and that **SR-12 / SR-13** leave brand and IP ownership `DECISION REQUIRED`** | **Any brand valuation, any licence fee, any royalty rate, any statement of who owns the name (**`FC-E4`, **OPT-H**'s *"brand licence, related-party agreements and strict transfer-pricing discipline [V]"**)** | **IP counsel + valuer + tax counsel** |
| **HC-10** | **Governance and audit capital** — amendment, validation, audit, records and the professional advice this brief keeps requiring | **`NOBODY — funding-map row 14`** | **That the corpus's own funding map identifies the row and leaves it unfunded; that **this brief is itself an instance of the cost**** | **Any fee estimate, any audit scope, any statement that an audit is or is not required (**CEA `AU-1 … AU-12`: all `NO POPULATION`**)** | **Chartered accountant + statutory auditor + governance counsel** |
| **HC-11** | **Risk and insurance capital** — cover for custody of goods, safety incidents, professional error, cyber and data, employment liability | **`UNKNOWN`** | **That the requirement is `[SOURCE]` (**SR-04**, **L1 … L15**, **PP-06**) and that **no coverage exists or is claimed** (**CEA `PSC-*`, Part XVI here**)** | **Any coverage, any premium, any policy wording, any statement that an activity is insurable — **`PP-06`: insurance status is `UNKNOWN`, and L4 asks whether protection and first-response work is insurable at all**** | **Insurance broker + liability counsel** |
| **HC-12** | **Records and data capital** — the means of capturing the seventeen record types with **WHO → WHAT → WHEN → WHY → AMOUNT → AUTHORITY → EVIDENCE → STATUS** | **Not specified; **paper is sufficient**** | **That the requirement exists, that paper satisfies it, and that **ID-13** is one of only two CEA dependency steps not blocked — so this can start today** | **Any system, any software, any retention period (**`SR-06`, **DR-9**, **PVR-20** are `[UNKNOWN]` and require counsel**)** | **Internal control design + chartered accountant + data-protection counsel** |
| **HC-13** | **Mission capital** — the pre-surplus instrument that lets service operate before `S-6` | **`NOBODY`** | **That the mission currently waits for commerce, which inverts the organisation's stated purpose; that CEA **`MI-1`** exists to name the gap and is **`PROPOSED`, not approved**** | **Any amount, any funding source, any statement that donations may fund it (**CEA: *"donations may be possible **after validation**"* — validation has not occurred, and restricted money carries an obligation to honour the restriction**)** | **Governance decision + tax counsel + charitable-regulation counsel** |
| **HC-14** | **Failure capital** — containment, remediation, restitution, professional advice and decision time for the eighteen failure modes | **`NOBODY`** | **That six failure modes are already open (**F-05, F-08, F-10, F-11, F-14, F-17**) and that each is unfunded** | **Any provision, any contingency figure, any insurance recovery assumption, any statement that a failure is covered** | **Chartered accountant + liability counsel + insurance broker** |
| **HC-15** | **Decision capital — `PROFESSIONAL INPUT — NOT CURRENT ARCHITECTURE DECISION`** — the cost, in elapsed time and in the attention of the only people who can decide, of every question this architecture defers | **The founder and the eleven role-holders, personally and invisibly** | **That **CEA §XXIV.5** identifies decision latency as an economic cost; that **U-PD-16** shows six instruments performed `SR-01`'s next action without `SR-01` advancing; that **DA-31** prohibits a successor instrument — so the cost is rising while the permitted responses narrow** | **Any quantification of the cost, any statement that delay has caused loss, and **any** implication that further analysis is a substitute for a decision. **This row exists to price the deferral, not to extend it**** | **No discipline can supply it. **It is discharged only by the human act named at §XXX.5**** |

## XII.7 What must not be invented — the eight named prohibitions

**[P]** Mandate §13, adopted item by item. **Each is listed with its ground and with the identifier that already governs it, so that no future instrument can reintroduce it by omission.**

| Prohibited invention | **Ground** | **The identifier that already forbids it** |
|---|---|---|
| **A founder salary** | **No employment or service agreement exists; no entity exists to be the employer; the approver would be the beneficiary** | **CEA **XV.4 row 2**; **CF-16**; **SR-20** `AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED (beneficiary recusal)`; **V-08**** |
| **A dividend** | **No share capital, no distributable profits, no owner** | **CEA **XV.5**; **PVR-09**; **GC-01**; **`FC-E2`, `FC-E3`**** |
| **A profit share** | **No profit exists, and a profit share to a leader is prohibited on the same reasoning as `MLM-2`** | **CEA **SEP-6**; **[S-D:2999]**; **J-09**, **J-10**, **J-12**** |
| **A fee** | **No counterparty with capacity to pay or receive; **`H-Q17`** asks whether Sewak Sathi may pay Krytos at all and the answer is `UNKNOWN`** | **CEA **XV.6**; **GD-02**; **PVR-09**; **[S-K(E):854]**** |
| **A licence** | **No owner of any mark, name or work is established** | **`SR-12`, `SR-13`**; **`FC-E4`**; **HC-9**; **OPT-H**'s *"brand licence … [V]"*** |
| **A royalty** | **Same, and a royalty presupposes a valuation and a licensee** | **`SR-12`, `SR-13`**; **HC-8**, **HC-9**** |
| **A transfer** | **All ten pairings at §XII.4 are blocked, impossible, prohibited or a category error** | **CEA **XV.4**; **C-15**; **PP-04**; **A-N16**; **T-1 … T-5**** |
| **An agreement** | **An agreement requires two persons with capacity. **`FA-4`**: no constitutive document exists on either side of any pairing that involves Krytos** | **CEA **SEP-8**; **EA-26**; **Rule K-4**; **DA-30**** |

# PART XIII — COMPENSATION: TWENTY-ONE DIMENSIONS

## XIII.1 The one settled thing, and the twenty that are not

**[P]** Mandate §15. **Twenty-one dimensions of compensation are examined. Exactly one of them is settled, and it is settled as a prohibition rather than as an entitlement.**

> ### **`THE BASIS OF COMPENSATION IS SETTLED: VERIFIED WORK, AND NOTHING ELSE.`**
>
> **"Compensation attaches to verified work, not to people. There is no head, no downline and no count in the payment chain" **[S: App. A §14.3]**, **[S: Ch.19 step 1]**, **[S-C: §22.2 point 1]** — CEA **§V.6 row 1**, **RT-06**, **EA-13**.**
>
> ### **`EVERY OTHER DIMENSION IS OPEN, AND NO COMPENSATION MAY BE PAID TODAY.`**
>
> **`C-15`: *"Any external payment"* is prohibited — *"no external payment can lawfully or operationally be made by or to this architecture"* **[S-I: §XIII.3]**. **`PP-04`**, **`A-N16`**, **T-1 … T-5 = 0 of 5**. **The formula itself does not exist: *"the formula does not exist"* **[S-B: PC-01]** — CEA **GF-3**, **V-01 … V-03**.**

## XIII.3 The count, and what it means

| Finding | **Result** |
|---|---|
| **Dimensions examined** | **21** |
| **PRIMARY class — `1` answerable now** | **3 · `CD-06`, `CD-20`, `CD-21`** — and two of the three are prohibitions rather than entitlements |
| **PRIMARY class — `2` requires an entity** | **2 · `CD-03`, `CD-15`** |
| **PRIMARY class — `4` tax and accounting question** | **4 · `CD-05`, `CD-13`, `CD-16`, `CD-17`** |
| **PRIMARY class — `5` labour and employment question** | **5 · `CD-01`, `CD-04`, `CD-12`, `CD-14`, `CD-18`** |
| **PRIMARY class — `8` founder decision required** | **1 · `CD-10`** |
| **PRIMARY class — `9` governance decision required** | **3 · `CD-07`, `CD-11`, `CD-19`** |
| **PRIMARY class — `10` cannot yet be answered** | **3 · `CD-02`, `CD-08`, `CD-09`** |
| **Primary classes sum** | **3 + 2 + 4 + 5 + 1 + 3 + 3 = 21 · `EXACTLY 21, ONE PRIMARY CLASS PER DIMENSION`** |
| **PRIMARY classes not used** | **`3` requires a constitutive document · `6` insurance and liability · `7` factual evidence — **none is a primary class here, although `CD-18` carries a secondary class `6`, `CD-07` and `CD-15` carry secondary `7`, `CD-02` carries secondary `2`, `CD-04` and `CD-09` carry secondary `8`, `CD-14` and `CD-16` carry secondary `5` or `4`, and `CD-17` and `CD-19` carry secondary `4`. **Secondary classifications are recorded so that no dimension is routed to only one professional, and they are the reason §V.3's class counts do not equal 21**** |
| **Entity dependency** | **14 dimensions fully entity-dependent · 3 partly **(`CD-11`, `CD-17`, `CD-19` — designable now, operable only after an entity)** · 4 not entity-dependent **(`CD-06`, `CD-07`, `CD-20`, `CD-21`)**. **14 + 3 + 4 = 21**** |
| **Dimensions that can be worked on today without an entity** | **7 — but only **`CD-21`** is *executable* today. **`CD-06`** and **`CD-20`** are already settled rules and need no work; **`CD-07`, `CD-11`, `CD-17`, `CD-19`** can be **designed** on paper now and cannot **operate** until `SR-01` and `SR-03` return** |
| **Dimensions in which this brief states a number** | **0** — **`V-01 … V-03`, `V-07`, `V-08`, `V-11`, `V-12` remain empty, and every statutory figure is `CURRENT-LAW PROFESSIONAL VERIFICATION REQUIRED`** |

> ### **`TWENTY-ONE DIMENSIONS. ONE SETTLED AS AN ENTITLEMENT BASIS. TWO SETTLED AS PROHIBITIONS. EIGHTEEN OPEN. FIFTEEN WAIT ON AN ENTITY THAT DOES NOT EXIST. ONE — `CD-21`, THE RECORD — CAN BE STARTED TODAY ON PAPER.`**

# PART XIV — TAX MATRIX: NINETEEN HEADS

## XIV.1 The rule that governs this Part

**[P]** Mandate §16, §24, §25. **Three rules are stated before any head is examined, because two of them are the reason this Part contains no advice.**

| Rule | **Content** |
|---|---|
| **TX-1** | **Every head is classified into exactly one of four statuses — `ANSWERABLE NOW` · `REQUIRES ENTITY` · `REQUIRES FACTS` · `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL OPINION` — and where more than one applies, the *governing* status is the one that must be discharged first** |
| **TX-2** | **No statutory rate, threshold, percentage, limit, deadline or interval is stated as fact. Where the corpus states one, this brief cites the corpus location and marks it **`CURRENT-LAW PROFESSIONAL VERIFICATION REQUIRED`**. Mandate §24** |
| **TX-3** | **The corpus's own tax statements are `[X]` — external, web-sourced — and the corpus itself forbids treating them as conclusions: *"No economic, legal or tax conclusion is drawn from any of them in this document; each requires confirmation by a qualified professional for the actual entity, activity and jurisdiction [V]"* **[S-B:4010, Annexure D preamble]**, *"No legal conclusion is drawn from any of them in this document"* **[S-A:2354, Annexure C preamble]**. **Nothing here upgrades them. Mandate §25: no web-based legal certainty** |

> ### **`THE SINGLE MOST CONSEQUENTIAL TAX FACT IN THE CORPUS`**
>
> ***"`[V] REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL LEGAL AND TAX VALIDATION — the entity structure is [S: Ch.33] UNRESOLVED, so the applicable regime is not determined here."*** **[S-D: §36.5, line 3930]**
>
> **The corpus reached this conclusion itself, in its own words, six instruments ago. **Every head below inherits it: the applicable tax regime is a *function of* `SR-01`, and `SR-01` is `UNRESOLVED`. This is why sixteen of the nineteen heads carry `REQUIRES ENTITY` as their governing status.**

# PART XV — ACCOUNTING MATRIX: EIGHTEEN HEADS

## XV.1 The rule that governs this Part

> ### **`THIS BRIEF PRESCRIBES NO ACCOUNTING STANDARD, NO FRAMEWORK, NO BASIS AND NO PRESENTATION.`**
>
> **Mandate §17. **The choice of framework is a professional determination that depends on the entity form, which does not exist (**FA-4**, **`SR-01`**), and on the applicable law, which the corpus itself declines to determine: *"the entity structure is [S: Ch.33] UNRESOLVED, so the applicable regime is not determined here"* **[S-D: §36.5]**. **To name a standard here would be to do exactly what this brief exists to stop.**

| Rule | **Content** |
|---|---|
| **ACR-1** | **No standard, framework, basis, presentation format, disclosure schedule or reporting periodicity is named as the one to use. Where a framework is mentioned at all it appears only as a **question for the professional**** |
| **ACR-2** | **No accounting number is stated — no amount, rate, threshold, useful life, depreciation basis, provision, allowance or accrual figure. Mandate §24** |
| **ACR-3** | **The corpus's own accounting questions are reproduced verbatim where they exist, and are attributed to **[S-B:3995 EV-1]**, which already grouped them for a single instruction to *"Accountant / auditor"*** |
| **ACR-4** | **`NO POPULATION` is never restated as `NO EXCEPTIONS`. CEA **AF-2**: all twelve audits return `NO POPULATION`** |
| **ACR-5** | **Paper is sufficient for every record in this Part. **CEA §XXI.1**; **no software may be designed, authorised or implied** — delta-audit brief §18** |

| **AH-08** | **Restricted-fund accounting for the social-impact contribution** | **CEA **REC-14**; **`FC-11`** — *"segregated ledger"* **[S: App. A §20.2]**; **[S: Ch.20]**; **CEA **BP-1 … BP-5**; **`PVR-10`, `PVR-11`** | **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL OPINION`** · **`REQUIRES ENTITY`** | **`HIGH`** | **That the corpus already imposes the restriction — mission money may not fund operating salaries, venture bailouts, or political or religious activity — and that **`REC-14`** carries the restriction as a record field** | **Chartered accountant + tax counsel** |
| **AH-09** | **Direct-cost attribution rule, and the variable / L1-cost boundary** | **[S-B:3995 EV-1]** — *"direct-cost attribution rule (E-16)"*; **CEA **AG-02**, **REC-05**; **`G-05`** expense shifting; **GD-07 RI-3**** | **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL OPINION`** · **`REQUIRES FACTS`** | **`MEDIUM`** | **That the boundary is where **`G-05`** lives, that **CEA §X.7** names **RI-3** as the candidate that widens it, and that a written attribution rule is the only available control** | **Chartered accountant + internal control design** |

| **AH-13** | **Capitalisation versus expensing of capability and technology spend** | **CEA **PVR-18**, **HC-8**, **GD-07 RI-3**; **`SR-77`** — a payment system is *"Tier 0 because it is a refusal"*; **EA-21** | **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL OPINION`** | **`HIGH`** | **That **no software may be built or authorised** — delta-audit §18, CEA **§XXI.1** — so the question is presently hypothetical, and that **RI-3** would make it real** | **Chartered accountant + tax counsel** |

Assurance must convert from examination to sampling plus anomaly detection"*; **[S-A:2343 V7]**, **[S-B:4002 EV-8]** | **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL OPINION`** · **`REQUIRES ENTITY`** | **`HIGH`** | **That **all twelve audits currently return `NO POPULATION`, which is not `NO EXCEPTIONS`** — **AF-2** — and that **`AU-11`** can never be internal, because independence is unachievable for founder compensation with eleven people** | **Statutory auditor + chartered accountant** |
| **AH-18** | **Consolidation, and whether more than one reporting entity will exist** | **CEA **`OPT-I`** parent/holding + operating — *"the only option the corpus describes as the present conceptual state"* — blocked by **UE-24**; **`OPT-H`** hybrid; **`FC-E7`**, **`FC-E8`; [S-A:2337 V1]** — *"entity form under Krytos … dissolution and restructuring"*** | **`REQUIRES ENTITY`** | **`TOTAL`** | **That **`FA-2`** is an assertion and not a holding relationship, that **`SEP-2`** is `SEPARATED — RELATIONSHIP `UNKNOWN — DECISION REQUIRED``, and that no consolidation question can arise before at least two legal persons exist** | **Chartered accountant + corporate counsel** |

|---|---|---|

| **`REQUIRES ENTITY`** *(governing)* | **AH-01, AH-12, AH-15, AH-17, AH-18** | **5** |

| **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL OPINION`** *(governing)* | **AH-02, AH-03, AH-04, AH-05, AH-06, AH-07, AH-08, AH-09, AH-11, AH-13** | **10** |

## XV.4 The one head that can be worked on today

**[P]** **AH-16 is the only accounting head whose *design* does not wait on `SR-01`.** It is stated separately because it is the highest-value zero-cost action available anywhere in this brief, and because it is also the precondition of every other head: nothing can be accounted for, audited, taxed or defended without a record.

| Element | **Content** |
|---|---|
| **The eight fields** | **`WHO → WHAT → WHEN → WHY → AMOUNT → AUTHORITY → EVIDENCE → STATUS`** — CEA **§XXI.1**, **REC-01 … REC-17** |
| **The medium** | **Paper. **CEA §XXI.1**; delta-audit §18; **no system, no software, no platform, no spreadsheet requirement**** |
| **What may be entered today** | **Every field except `AMOUNT` and `AUTHORITY`, which stay `UNKNOWN` and `UNASSIGNED` respectively in **9 of 17** record types** |
| **What may not be entered** | **Any figure, any authority, any retention period, any standard reference, any classification** |
| **Why it matters before an entity exists** | **Because the record is the evidence that **`CD-21`**, **TX-12**, **AH-05**, **AH-09**, **AH-10**, **AH-14** and **AH-15** all require, and because a record created later is worth less than a record created contemporaneously. **CEA `G-14`** — evidence fabrication — is only detectable against a contemporaneous population** |
| **Its dependency** | **None. **CEA **`ID-13`** is one of only two dependency steps not blocked, and this is the other one's twin**** |

## XV.5 Required professional output — accounting

```
QUESTION
  For each of the eighteen heads at §XV.2: which framework and basis apply in each candidate
  legal form, what does the head require the record to carry, and which heads are unavailable
  or materially different in some forms?

PROFESSIONAL DISCIPLINE
  Chartered accountant (all eighteen) · statutory auditor (AH-17) · tax counsel (AH-04,
  AH-06, AH-07, AH-08, AH-13, AH-14, AH-15) · labour counsel (AH-05, AH-12) ·
  corporate counsel (AH-01, AH-15, AH-18) · valuer (AH-10, AH-14) ·
  records / archival specialist and data-protection counsel (AH-16) ·
  internal control design (AH-03, AH-09, AH-16)

CURRENT ARCHITECTURAL STATUS
  [SOURCE] [S-B:3995 EV-1] · [S-B:3997 EV-3] · [S-B:2534 NL-1] · [S-A:2343 V7] ·
  [S-A:2348 V12] · [S-B:4002 EV-8] · [S-D: §36.5] · CEA Parts IX, X, XI, XIV, XVI, XXI,
  XXII · PVR-04, PVR-05, PVR-10, PVR-11, PVR-18, PVR-20 · REC-01 … REC-17 · DR-1 … DR-9 ·
  AU-1 … AU-12 · AF-2 · AG-02 · AG-10 · FM-1, FM-2 · G-01 … G-16 · OC-10 · SR-06 ·
  SR-19 · ID-13 · OPT-H, OPT-I · UE-24 · FC-04 · FC-11 · CF-06 · [S: App. A §20.2]

FACTS REQUIRED
  Whether any transaction of any kind has ever occurred · whether any receipt has ever been
  issued or received · whether any invoice exists · whether any asset has ever been
  contributed, by whom, and what became of it · whether any record of any kind already
  exists, in any medium · whether any person has kept any informal account · the eleven
  role-holders and their actual activities

DOCUMENTS REQUIRED
  CEA Parts IX, XXI, XXII · this Part · [S-B: Annexure C] · [S-A: Annexure B] ·
  SR-06's own evidence requirement · the FC-01 … FC-33 list at §IX.3

ENTITY DEPENDENCY
  YES as a matter of framework for 5 of 18 heads · NO for AH-16's record design ·
  and every head's ANSWER requires a professional opinion regardless of entity status

PROFESSIONAL DETERMINATION
  [TO BE COMPLETED BY A QUALIFIED INDIAN CHARTERED ACCOUNTANT — including the framework,
   the basis, and the retention period. This brief names none of them]

CONDITIONS / ASSUMPTIONS
  [TO BE COMPLETED BY THE PROFESSIONAL]

CONSEQUENCE FOR CURRENT ARCHITECTURE
  Determines the content of REC-01 … REC-17 · determines whether REV-1 … REV-4 can be computed
  at all · determines whether AU-1 … AU-12 can ever return anything other than NO POPULATION ·
  and determines whether AH-18 will ever arise

NEXT DEPENDENCY
  AH-16 can start today with no dependency. AH-01 depends on SR-01. Every other head
  depends on AH-01 and on AH-16
```
